128        1/1                instruction[15:8]  = mem[pc + 1];
129        1/1                instruction[23:16] = mem[pc + 2];
130        1/1                instruction[31:24] = mem[pc + 3];
131        1/1                $display("Instruction: %h",instruction);
132        1/1                instruction_str.hextoa(instruction);
133        1/1                while (instruction_str.len() <= 7) begin
134        1/1                   instruction_str = {"0",instruction_str};
135                           end
                   ==>  WHILE_FALSE
136        1/1                exe_inst_queue.push_back(instruction_str);
137                           //$display({"Instruction: ",instruction_str});
138                           //Decode and Execute
139        1/1                opcode = instruction[6:0];
140        1/1                case (opcode)
141                              7'b0110111 : begin //LUI
142        0/1     ==>              imm2 = instruction[31:12];
143        0/1     ==>              rd  = instruction[11:7];
144        0/1     ==>              reg_file[rd] = {imm2,12'b0000_0000_0000};
145                              end
146                              7'b0010111 : begin //AUIPC
147        0/1     ==>              imm2 = instruction[31:12];
148        0/1     ==>              rd  = instruction[11:7];

150                              end
151                              7'b1101111 : begin //JAL
152        0/1     ==>              offset = {instruction[31],instruction[19:12],instruction[20],instruction[30:21],1'b0};

154        0/1     ==>              rd  = instruction[11:7];
155        0/1     ==>              ext_imm = 32'(signed'(offset));
156        0/1     ==>              reg_file[rd] = pc + 4;
157        0/1     ==>              next_pc = pc + ext_imm;
158        0/1     ==>              update_pc = 1;
159                              end
160                              7'b1100111 : begin //JALR
161        0/1     ==>              imm = instruction[31:20];

163        0/1     ==>              rd  = instruction[11:7];
164        0/1     ==>              reg_file[rd] = pc + 4;
165        0/1     ==>              ext_imm = 32'(signed'(imm));
166        0/1     ==>              next_pc = reg_file[rs1] + $signed(ext_imm);
167        0/1     ==>              next_pc[0] = 0;
168        0/1     ==>              update_pc = 1;
169                              end
170                              7'b1100011 : begin //BEQ, BNE, BLT, BGE, BLTU, BGEU

174        0/1     ==>                    rs2 = instruction[24:20];
175        0/1     ==>                    rs1 = instruction[19:15];
176        0/1     ==>                    offset_b  = {instruction[31],instruction[7],instruction[30:25],instruction[11:8],1'b0};
177        0/1     ==>                    ext_imm  = 32'(signed'(offset_b));
178        0/1     ==>                    if(reg_file[rs1] == reg_file[rs2]) begin
179        0/1     ==>                       next_pc = pc + $signed(ext_imm);
180        0/1     ==>                       update_pc = 1;
181                                       end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
182        0/1     ==>                    rd = 0;
183                                    end
184                                    3'b001 : begin //BNE

33         1/1                load_RTL_data(file2, inst_cont, data2,data_csr);
34         1/1                foreach(data2[i]) begin
35         1/1                  results.push_back(data2[i]);
36                            end
37         1/1                foreach(data_csr[i]) begin
38         1/1                  results_csr.push_back(data_csr[i]);
39                            end
40                            //$display("size_p",prediction.size());
41                            //$display("size_r",results.size());
42                          end
43                          
44         1/1              compare(prediction,results,exe_inst_queue);
45         1/1              compare_csr(csr_predict,results_csr,exe_inst_queue);
46         1/1              $display("---------------------------------------",);
47         1/1              $display("----------------TEST PASS--------------");
48         1/1              $display("---------------------------------------");
49                      
50                        endtask : selfcheck
51                      
52                        task compare(input string str_queue1 [$],
53                                     input string str_queue2 [$],
54                                     input string exe_inst_queue [$]); //prediction,results,instruction number
55                          
56                          string str1;
57                          string str2;
58                          string convert;
59                          int count;
60                          int pc;
61                          int prev_pc;
62                          int pc_aux;
63                          int file;
64                          int register;
65                          bit fail;
66                          logic [31:0] pc_offset;
67                          //$display("size",str_queue1.size());
68         1/1              count = 0;
69         1/1              prev_pc = 0;
70         1/1              pc_aux = 0;
71         1/1              fail = 0;
72         1/1              pc_offset = 32'h10054;
73         1/1              file = $fopen("comparison.txt","w");
74         1/1              $fwrite(file,"---Prediction--RTL Result----\n");
75         1/1              if ((str_queue1.size() == 0) && (str_queue2.size() == 0)) begin
76         0/1     ==>        $display("Some data is missing");
77         0/1     ==>        $display("Prediction Queue Size: %d",str_queue1.size());
78         0/1     ==>        $display("RTL Results Queue Size: %d",str_queue2.size());
79         0/1     ==>        $display("---------------------------------------",);
80         0/1     ==>        $display("----------------TEST FAIL--------------");
81         0/1     ==>        $display("---------------------------------------",);
82         0/1     ==>        $finish;
83                          end
                        MISSING_ELSE
84         1/1              while((str_queue1.size() != 0) && (str_queue2.size() != 0)) begin
85                            //$display("size",str_queue1.size());
86                            //$display("size",str_queue2.size());
87         1/1                str1 = str_queue1.pop_front();
88         1/1                str2 = str_queue2.pop_front();
89                      
90                            //$display({"DEBUG: ",str1});
91                            //$display({"DEBUG: ",str2});
92         1/1                if(str2 == "xxxxxxxx") begin
93         0/1     ==>          convert.itoa(count);
94                              //$display("Uninitialized register: x", convert);
95                            end // if(str1 == "xxxxxxxx")
96         1/1                else if(str1 != str2) begin
97                              //$display("DEBUG: %d",count);
98         0/1     ==>          if((count % 32) == 0) begin
99         0/1     ==>            $display({"Expected PC result: ",str1});
100                               //$display("Expected PC wihtout offset: %h",(str1.atohex() + pc_offset));
101        0/1     ==>            $display({"RTL PC result: ",str2});
102        0/1     ==>            fail = 1;
103                             end
104                             else begin
105        0/1     ==>            convert.itoa(count%32);
106                               //$display("There has been a mismatch at instruction located at PC = %h (Wihtout offset: %h)",prev_pc,(prev_pc + pc_offset));
107        0/1     ==>            $display("There has been a mismatch at instruction located at PC = %h",prev_pc);
108        0/1     ==>            $display({"Expected x",convert," result: ",str1});
109        0/1     ==>            $display({"RTL x",convert," result: ",str2});
110        0/1     ==>            fail = 1;
111                             end
112                           end
                        MISSING_ELSE
113                           //$display("DEBUG: %d",count);
114        1/1                if((count % 32) == 0) begin
115        1/1                  $fwrite(file,{"\nInstruction: ",exe_inst_queue[count/32],"\n"});
116        1/1                  prev_pc = str1.atohex();
117        1/1                  $fwrite(file,{"PC: ",str1,", ",str2,"\n"});
118                           end // if((count % 32) == 0)
119                           else begin
120        1/1                  register = count % 32;
121        1/1                  convert.itoa(register);
122        1/1                  $fwrite(file,{"x",convert,": ",str1,", ",str2,"\n"});
123                           end
124        1/1                if (fail == 1) begin
125        0/1     ==>          $display("---------------------------------------",);
126        0/1     ==>          $display("----------------TEST FAIL--------------");
127        0/1     ==>          $display("---------------------------------------",);
128        0/1     ==>          $finish;
129                           end // if (fail == 1)
                        MISSING_ELSE
130        1/1                count++;
131                         end
                   ==>  WHILE_FALSE
132        1/1              $fclose(file);
133                       
134                       endtask : compare
135                     
136                       task compare_csr(input string str_queue1 [$],
137                                        input string str_queue2 [$],
138                                        input string exe_inst_queue [$]); //prediction,results,instruction number
139                         
140                         string str1;
141                         string str2;
142                         string convert;
143                         int count;
144                         int pc;
145                         int prev_pc;
146                         int pc_aux;
147                         int file;
148                         int register;
149                         bit fail;
150                         logic [31:0] pc_offset;
151                         //$display("size",str_queue1.size());
152        1/1              count = 0;
153        1/1              prev_pc = 0;
154        1/1              pc_aux = 0;
155        1/1              fail = 0;
156        1/1              pc_offset = 32'h10054;
157        1/1              file = $fopen("comparison_csr.txt","w");
158        1/1              $fwrite(file,"---Prediction--RTL Result----\n");
159        1/1              while((str_queue1.size() != 0) && (str_queue2.size() != 0)) begin
160                           //$display("size1 %d",str_queue1.size());
161                           //$display("size2 %d",str_queue2.size());
162        1/1                str1 = str_queue1.pop_front();
163        1/1                str2 = str_queue2.pop_front();
164        1/1                if(str2 == "xxxxxxxx") begin
165        0/1     ==>          convert.itoa(count);
166                           end // if(str1 == "xxxxxxxx")
167        1/1                else if(str1 != str2) begin
168        0/1     ==>          if((count % 13) == 0) begin
169        0/1     ==>            $display({"CSR: Expected PC result: ",str1});
170        0/1     ==>            $display({"CSR: RTL PC result: ",str2});
171        0/1     ==>            fail = 1;
172                             end
173                             else begin
174        0/1     ==>            convert.itoa((count%13)-1);
175                               //$display("There has been a mismatch at instruction located at PC = %h (Wihtout offset: %h)",prev_pc,(prev_pc + pc_offset));
176        0/1     ==>            $display("There has been a mismatch at instruction located at PC = %h",prev_pc);
177        0/1     ==>            $display({"Expected CSR",convert," result: ",str1});
178        0/1     ==>            $display({"RTL CSR",convert," result: ",str2});
179        0/1     ==>            fail = 1;
180                             end
181                           end
                        MISSING_ELSE
182        1/1                if((count % 13) == 0) begin
183        1/1                  $fwrite(file,{"\nInstruction: ",exe_inst_queue[count/32],"\n"});
184        1/1                  prev_pc = str1.atohex();
185        1/1                  $fwrite(file,{"PC: ",str1,", ",str2,"\n"});
186                           end // if((count % 32) == 0)
187                           else begin
188        1/1                  register = (count%13)-1;
189        1/1                  convert.itoa(register);
190        1/1                  $fwrite(file,{"CSR",convert,": ",str1,", ",str2,"\n"});
191                           end
192        1/1                if (fail == 1) begin
193        0/1     ==>          $display("---------------------------------------",);
194        0/1     ==>          $display("----------------TEST FAIL--------------");
195        0/1     ==>          $display("---------------------------------------",);
196        0/1     ==>          $finish;
197                           end // if (fail == 1)
                        MISSING_ELSE
198        1/1                count++;
199                         end
                   ==>  WHILE_FALSE
200        1/1              $fclose(file);
201                       
202                       endtask : compare_csr
203                     
204                       task load_RTL_data(input string str, input int inst_num, output string data[$], output string data_csr[$]);
205                         int out;

209                         string line;
210                         string info;

213        1/1              data_csr.delete();
214        1/1              count = 0;
215        1/1              prev_pc = 0;
216        1/1              out = $fopen(str,"r");
217                         //$display("DEBUG Inst_num: %d", inst_num);
218        1/1              while(!$feof(out)) begin
219        1/1                $fgets(line,out);
220        1/1                if({line[0],line[1],line[2]} == "PC:") begin
221        1/1                  info = {line[4],line[5],line[6],line[7],line[8],line[9],line[10],line[11]};

225                             //$display("count: %d",count);
226        1/1                  if (pc != prev_pc) begin
227        1/1                    count++;
228        1/1                    prev_pc = pc;
229                             end // if ()
                        MISSING_ELSE
230                             /*else if (pc == 0) begin
231                               count++;
232                             end*/
233        1/1                  if (count == (inst_num - 1)) begin //registers for current instruction found
234                               //$display({"PC: ",info});
235        1/1                    data.push_back(info);
236        1/1                    data_csr.push_back(info);
237        1/1                    $fgets(line,out);
238        1/1                    while ({line[0],line[1],line[2],line[3],line[4]} != "x1,ra") begin //discard lines until you find x1 line
239        1/1                      $fgets(line,out); //discard line
240        1/1                      if($feof(out)) begin
241        0/1     ==>                $display("ERROR in RTL File");

                   ==>  WHILE_FALSE

387                       task load_expected_data(input string str, input int inst_num, input logic [32:0] status_in[0:31], output string data_out[$], output logic [32:0] status_out[0:31]);
388                     
389                         int out;
390                         int count;
391                         logic [31:0] pc;
392                         logic [31:0] pc_offset;
393                         logic [31:0] instruction;
394                         string line;
395                         string info;
396                         string instruction_str;
397                         string data [$];
398                         pc_offset = 32'h10054;
399                         data.delete();
400                         data_out.delete();
401                         status_out = status_in;
402                         count = 0;
403                         out = $fopen(str,"r");
404                         while(!$feof(out)) begin
405                           $fgets(line,out);
406                           if({line[0],line[1]} == "00") begin
407                             count++;
408                             //$display("DEBUG: ",info);
409                             if(count == inst_num)begin //registers for current instruction found
410                               instruction_str = {line[40],line[41],line[42],line[43],line[44],line[45],line[46],line[47]};
411                               instruction = instruction_str.atohex();
412                               $display("Instruction: %h",instruction);
413                               info = {line[30],line[31],line[32],line[33],line[34],line[35],line[36],line[37]};
414                               pc = info.atohex();
415                               pc = pc - pc_offset;
416                               //$display("PC: %h",pc);
417                               info.hextoa(pc);
418                               //$display("DEBUG: ",info);
419                               while (info.len() <= 7) begin
420                                 info = {"0",info};
421                               end // while (info.size() <= 7)
422                               //$display("DEBUG: ",info);
423                               //$display({"PC: ",info});
424                               data.push_back(info);
425                               $fgets(line,out); //x1 line
426                               info = {line[12],line[13],line[14],line[15],line[16],line[17],line[18],line[19]};
427                               data.push_back(info);
428                               $fgets(line,out); //x2 line && x3 line
429                               info = {line[12],line[13],line[14],line[15],line[16],line[17],line[18],line[19]};
430                               data.push_back(info);
431                               info = {line[33],line[34],line[35],line[36],line[37],line[38],line[39],line[40]};
432                               data.push_back(info);
433                               $fgets(line,out); //x4 line && x5 line
434                               info = {line[12],line[13],line[14],line[15],line[16],line[17],line[18],line[19]};
435                               data.push_back(info);
436                               info = {line[33],line[34],line[35],line[36],line[37],line[38],line[39],line[40]};
437                               data.push_back(info);
438                               $fgets(line,out); //x6 line && x7 line
439                               info = {line[12],line[13],line[14],line[15],line[16],line[17],line[18],line[19]};
440                               data.push_back(info);

493                               $fgets(line,out); //x28 line && x29 line
494                               info = {line[12],line[13],line[14],line[15],line[16],line[17],line[18],line[19]};
495                               data.push_back(info);
496                               info = {line[33],line[34],line[35],line[36],line[37],line[38],line[39],line[40]};
497                               data.push_back(info);
498                               $fgets(line,out); //x30 line && x31 line
499                               info = {line[12],line[13],line[14],line[15],line[16],line[17],line[18],line[19]};
500                               data.push_back(info);
501                               info = {line[33],line[34],line[35],line[36],line[37],line[38],line[39],line[40]};
502                               data.push_back(info);
503                               //$fgets(line,out); //discard line
504                               //$display("size_d",data.size());
505                               $fclose(out);
506                               //$display("size: %d",data.size());
507                               instruction_analysis(instruction,pc,data,status_in,data_out,status_out);
508                               //$display("size: %d",data.size());
509                               print_reg_file(data_out);
510                               break;
511                               //disable load_data;
512                               //$display(data[1]);
513                             end
514                           end // if(line[0] == x)
515                         end
516                         $fclose(out);

520                       task executed_inst_count(input string str, output int count);
521                     
522                         string line;
523                         string info;
524                         int out;
525                     
526                         count = 0;
527                         out = $fopen(str,"r");
528                         $fgets(line,out);
529                         while ({line[0],line[1],line[2],line[3]} != "TRAP") begin
530                           if ({line[0],line[1]} == "00") begin
531                             count++;
532                           end // if ({line[0],line[1]} == "00")
533                           $fgets(line,out);
534                           if($feof(out)) begin
535                             $display("ERROR in Predictor File");
536                             break;
537                           end
538                         end // while ()
539                         $fclose(out);
540                       endtask : executed_inst_count
541                     
542                       task last_inst_search(input string str, output logic [31:0] last_inst);
543                     
544                         string line;
545                         string info;
546                         int out;
547                         logic [31:0] instruction;
548                         logic [31:0] instruction_old;
549                     
550                         instruction = 0;
551                         instruction_old = 0;
552                         out = $fopen(str,"r");
553                         $fgets(line,out);
554                         while ({line[0],line[1],line[2],line[3]} != "TRAP") begin
555                           if ({line[0],line[1]} == "00") begin
556                             instruction_old = instruction;
557                             info = {line[40],line[41],line[42],line[43],line[44],line[45],line[46],line[47]};
558                             instruction = info.atohex();
559                             //$display("DEBUG %h",instruction);
560                           end
561                           $fgets(line,out);
562                           if($feof(out)) begin
563                             $display("ERROR in Predictor File");
564                             break;
565                           end
566                         end // while ()
567                         $fclose(out);
568                         last_inst = instruction_old;
569                         //$display("DEBUG %h",last_inst);
570                       endtask : last_inst_search
571                     
572                       task instruction_analysis(input logic [31:0] instruction, input logic [31:0] pc, input string data_in [$], input logic [32:0] status_in[0:31], output string data_out [$], output logic [32:0] status_out[0:31]);
573                     
574                         //Identify which instruction we are running
575                         // The jal, jalr and stores are important since they change according to the memory map
576                         int           register;
577                         logic  [31:0] next_pc;
578                         logic  [6:0]  opcode;
579                         string        reg_str;
580                         string        content_str;
581                     
582                         register = 0;
583                         next_pc = 0;
584                         data_out.delete();
585                         data_out = data_in;
586                         status_out = status_in;
587                         //$display("size: %d",data_in.size());
588                         //Checks if a JAL ocurred before
589                         foreach(status_in[i]) begin
590                           //$display("reg: %d",i);
591                           //$display("status: %b",status_in[i]);
592                           if(status_in[i][32] == 1) begin
593                             next_pc = status_in[i][31:0];
594                             register = i;
595                             reg_file_update(register,next_pc,data_in,data_out);
596                           end
597                         end
598                         //JAL instruction
599                         opcode = {instruction[6],instruction[5],instruction[4],instruction[3],instruction[2],instruction[1],instruction[0]};
600                         case (opcode)
601                           7'b1101111 : begin
602                             $display("JAL Instruction identified!");
603                             if({instruction[11],instruction[10],instruction[9],instruction[8],instruction[7]} != 5'b00000) begin //$zero register
604                               register = {instruction[11],instruction[10],instruction[9],instruction[8],instruction[7]};
605                               next_pc = pc + 4;
606                               //$display("pc: %h",pc);
607                               //$display("next_pc: %h",next_pc);
608                               status_out[register][31:0] = next_pc;
609                               status_out[register][32] = 1;
610                               reg_str.itoa(register);
611                               content_str.hextoa(next_pc);
612                               while (content_str.len() <= 7) begin
613                                 content_str = {"0",content_str};
614                               end // while (content_str.size() <= 7)
615                               $display("This JAL instruction have made a valid update that is different from the reference model!");
616                               $display({"Register x",reg_str," content needs to be ",content_str});
617                               //$display("status: %b",status_out[register]);
618                               reg_file_update(register,next_pc,data_in,data_out);
619                             end
620                           end
621                           7'b1100111 : begin
622                             $display("JALR Instruction identified!");
623                             if({instruction[11],instruction[10],instruction[9],instruction[8],instruction[7]} != 5'b00000) begin //$zero register
624                               register = {instruction[11],instruction[10],instruction[9],instruction[8],instruction[7]};
625                               next_pc = pc + 4;
626                               status_out[register][31:0] = next_pc;
627                               status_out[register][32] = 1;
628                               reg_str.itoa(register);
629                               content_str.hextoa(next_pc);
630                               while (content_str.len() <= 7) begin
631                                 content_str = {"0",content_str};
632                               end // while (content_str.size() <= 7)
633                               $display("This JALR instruction have made a valid update that is different from the reference model!");
634                               $display({"Register x",reg_str," content needs to be ",content_str});
635                               reg_file_update(register,next_pc,data_in,data_out);
636                             end
637                           //Check if another instruction is going to overwrite the an updated register that differs from reference model
638                           end
639                           7'b0110111 : begin
640                             register = {instruction[11],instruction[10],instruction[9],instruction[8],instruction[7]};
641                             if(status_out[register][32] == 1) begin
642                               status_out[register][32] = 0;
643                             end
644                           end
645                           7'b0010111 : begin
646                             register = {instruction[11],instruction[10],instruction[9],instruction[8],instruction[7]};
647                             if(status_out[register][32] == 1) begin
648                               status_out[register][32] = 0;
649                             end
650                           end
651                           7'b0000011 : begin
652                             register = {instruction[11],instruction[10],instruction[9],instruction[8],instruction[7]};
653                             if(status_out[register][32] == 1) begin
654                               status_out[register][32] = 0;
655                             end
656                           end
657                           7'b0010011 : begin
658                             register = {instruction[11],instruction[10],instruction[9],instruction[8],instruction[7]};
659                             if(status_out[register][32] == 1) begin
660                               status_out[register][32] = 0;
661                             end
662                           end
663                           7'b0110011 : begin
664                             register = {instruction[11],instruction[10],instruction[9],instruction[8],instruction[7]};
665                             if(status_out[register][32] == 1) begin
666                               status_out[register][32] = 0;
667                             end
668                           end
669                           7'b1110111 : begin
670                             register = {instruction[11],instruction[10],instruction[9],instruction[8],instruction[7]};
671                             if(status_out[register][32] == 1) begin
672                               status_out[register][32] = 0;
673                             end
674                           end
675                         endcase // opcode
676                     
677                       endtask : instruction_analysis
678                     
679                       task reg_file_update(input int register, input logic [31:0] content, input string data_in [$], output string data_out [$]);
680                         //This task updates the last content saved in the reference register_file
681                     
682                         int data_size;
683                         string content_str;
684                         string reg_str;
685                     
686                         reg_str.itoa(register);
687                         content_str.hextoa(content);
688                         while (content_str.len() <= 7) begin
689                           content_str = {"0",content_str};
690                         end // while (content_str.size() <= 7)
691                         data_out.delete();
692                         data_out = data_in;
693                         data_size = data_out.size();
694                         //$display({"DEBUG: ",content_str});
695                         data_out[data_size - (32 - register)] = content_str;
696                     
697                       endtask : reg_file_update
698                     
699                       task print_reg_file(input string data [$]);
700                     
701                         int queue_size;
702                     
703        1/1              queue_size = data.size();
704                         //$display("size: %d",queue_size);
705        1/1              $display({"PC: ",data[queue_size - 32]});
706        1/1              $display({"x1, ra: ",data[queue_size - 31]});
707        1/1              $display({"x2, sp: ",data[queue_size - 30]});
708        1/1              $display({"x3, gp: ",data[queue_size - 29]});
709        1/1              $display({"x4, tp: ",data[queue_size - 28]});
710        1/1              $display({"x5, t0: ",data[queue_size - 27]});
711        1/1              $display({"x6, t1: ",data[queue_size - 26]});
712        1/1              $display({"x7, t2: ",data[queue_size - 25]});
713        1/1              $display({"x8, s0: ",data[queue_size - 24]});
714        1/1              $display({"x9, s1: ",data[queue_size - 23]});
715        1/1              $display({"x10, a0: ",data[queue_size - 22]});
716        1/1              $display({"x11, a1: ",data[queue_size - 21]});
717        1/1              $display({"x12, a2: ",data[queue_size - 20]});
718        1/1              $display({"x13, a3: ",data[queue_size - 19]});
719        1/1              $display({"x14, a4: ",data[queue_size - 18]});
720        1/1              $display({"x15, a5: ",data[queue_size - 17]});
721        1/1              $display({"x16, a6: ",data[queue_size - 16]});
722        1/1              $display({"x17, a7: ",data[queue_size - 15]});
723        1/1              $display({"x18, s2: ",data[queue_size - 14]});
724        1/1              $display({"x19, s3: ",data[queue_size - 13]});
725        1/1              $display({"x20, s4: ",data[queue_size - 12]});
726        1/1              $display({"x21, s5: ",data[queue_size - 11]});
727        1/1              $display({"x22, s6: ",data[queue_size - 10]});
728        1/1              $display({"x23, s7: ",data[queue_size - 9]});
729        1/1              $display({"x24, s8: ",data[queue_size - 8]});
730        1/1              $display({"x25, s9: ",data[queue_size - 7]});
731        1/1              $display({"x26, s10: ",data[queue_size - 6]});
732        1/1              $display({"x27, s11: ",data[queue_size - 5]});
733        1/1              $display({"x28, t3: ",data[queue_size - 4]});
734        1/1              $display({"x29, t4: ",data[queue_size - 3]});
735        1/1              $display({"x30, t5: ",data[queue_size - 2]});
736        1/1              $display({"x31, t6: ",data[queue_size - 1]});
737        1/1              $display("------------------------------------\n");
738                       
739                       endtask : print_reg_file

755        1/1              $display({"CSR,full_shift: ",data[queue_size - 4]});
